records_processed = len(parse_lines)\n\n    # Duplicate detection (after records_processed known)\n    duplicate = False\n    try:\n        if args.retention_days <= 0:\n            duplicate = lines = normalized.split("\\n")\n        \n        # Strip trailing spaces and tabs from each line\n        lines = [line.rstrip(" \\t") for line in lines]\n        \n        # Remove trailing empty lines\n        while lines and lines[-1] == "":\n            lines.pop()\n        \n        # Join with newlines and append final newline\n        if lines:\n            canonical = "\\n".join(lines) + "\\n"\n        else:\n            canonical = ""\n        \n        return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()\n\n    def check_duplicate(self, file_hash: str) -> bool:\n        # conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            "SELECT timestamp FROM file_index WHERE file_hash = ?",\n            (file_hash,),\n        )\n        rows = cur.fetchall()\n        conn.close()\n        \n        for row in rows:\n            try:\n                ts = datetime.fromisoformat(row[0])\n                if ts >= cutoff:\n                    return True\n            except (ValueError, TypeError):\n                # Ignore rows with unparseable timestamps\n                continue\n        \n        return False\n\n    def record_file(self, file_hash: str, filename: str, record_count: int) -> None:\n        # Don\'t insert if retention disabled\n        if self.retention_days <= 0:\n            return\n            \n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            "INSERT INTO file_index (file_hash, filename, timestamp, record_count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",\n            (file_hash, filename, datetime.now().isoformat(), record_count),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n    def parse_and_validate_record(self, line: str, line_num: int) -> tuple[Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:\n        errors: List[str] = []\n        record: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        \n        record_length = self.schema["record_length"]\n        \n        # Record length validation\n        if len(line) < record_length:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Record too short (expected {record_length}, got {len(line)})")\n            return record, errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Record too long with non-whitespace content")\n                return record, errors\n            # Use only the first record_length characters\n            line = line[:record_length]\n\n        # Parse fields\n        for field in self.schema["fields"]:\n            name = field["name"]\n            start = int(field["start"])\n            length = int(field["length"])\n            required = bool(field.get("required", False))\n            ftype = field.get("type", "string")\n\n            raw = line[start : start + length]\n            value = raw.strip()\n            record[name] = value\n\n            # Required field check\n            if required and not value:\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is required")\n                continue\n\n            if not value:\n                continue\n\n            # Type-specific validation\n            if ftype == "string":\n                pattern = field.get("pattern")\n                if pattern and not re.match(pattern, value):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Field \'{name}\' does not match pattern")\n\n            elif ftype == "decimal":\n                try:\n                    amt = Decimal(value)\n                    if amt <= 0:\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} must be > 0")\n                    # Check decimal places\n                    if \'.\' in value:\n                        decimal_part = value.split(\'.\')[1]\n                        if len(decimal_part) > 2:\n {line_num}: {name} is not a valid decimal")\n\n            elif ftype == "date":\n                date_format = field.get("format", "%Y-%m-%d")\n                try:\n                    datetime.strptime(value, date_format)\n                except ValueError:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is not a valid date")\n\n        # Field-specific validations\n errors: List[str] = []\n\n        # eftno validation\n        if "eftno" in record and record["eftno"]:\n            eftno = record["eftno"]\n            if not eftno.isalnum():\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: eftno must be alphanumeric")\n\n        # bank_code validation\n        if "bank_code" in record and record["bank_code"]:\n            bank_code = errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no has forbidden prefix")\n                \n                # First 4 digits must not be only 0 and 1\n                if len(acct) >= 4:\n
